class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u0026lt;!-- In v4, this produces a SMALLER shadow than v3's \"shadow\" --\u0026gt;\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp class=\"pl-k\"\u003e@theme\u003c/span\u003e {\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e--font-sans\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"Inter\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e sans-serif;\n}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eVariables used for utility generation but NOT emitted as CSS variables.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWith inline modifier:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"@theme inline {\n  --font-sans: \u0026quot;SF Pro Text\u0026quot;, system-ui, sans-serif;\n  --color-primary: #0066cc;\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e@theme\u003c/span\u003e inline {\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e--font-sans\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"SF Pro Text\"\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e system-ui\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e sans-serif;\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e--color-primary\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e0066cc\u003c/span\u003e;\n}\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eVariables ARE emitted as CSS custom properties AND used for utilities.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOutput difference:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"/* @theme (no inline) - only utilities generated */\n.font-sans { font-family: Inter, sans-serif; }\n\n/* @theme inline - variables preserved */\n:root {\n  --font-sans: SF Pro Text, system-ui, sans-serif;\n  --color-primary: #0066cc;\n}\n.font-sans { font-family: var(--font-sans); }\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e/* @theme (no inline) - only utilities generated */\u003c/span\u003e\n.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003efont-sans\u003c/span\u003e { \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003efont-family\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e Inter\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e sans-serif; }\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e/* @theme inline - variables preserved */\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eroot\u003c/span\u003e {\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e--font-sans\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e SF Pro Text\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e system-ui\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e,\u003c/span\u003e sans-serif;\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e--color-primary\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-pds\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e0066cc\u003c/span\u003e;\n}\n.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003efont-sans\u003c/span\u003e { \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003efont-family\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003evar\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e--font-sans\u003c/span\u003e); }\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWhen to use inline:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eShadcn/Radix design systems that reference CSS vars\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDynamic theming with JavaScript\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSharing variables between Tailwind and custom CSS\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCombining with :root:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"@theme inline {\n  --color-background: var(--background);\n  --color-foreground: var(--foreground);\n}\n\n:root {\n  --background: #ffffff;\n  --foreground: #1d1d1f;\n}\n\n.dark {\n  --background: #000000;\n  --foreground: #f5f5f7;\n}\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003e@theme\u003c/span\u003e inline {\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e--color-background\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003evar\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e--background\u003c/span\u003e);\n  \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e--color-foreground\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-kos\"\u003e:\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003evar\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003e--foreground\u003c/span\u003e);\n}\n\n\u003cspan */\n@variant pointer-coarse (@media (pointer: coarse));\n\n/* Supports query */\n@variant supports-grid (@supports (display: grid));\n\n/* Dark mode with class strategy */\n@variant dark 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">Tailwind CSS v4 patterns for AI coding agents. CSS-first configuration, new directives (@theme, @utility, @variant, @source), migration from v3, and anti-patterns to avoid.</p>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Version</strong>: 0.1.0 | <strong>Status</strong>: verified | <strong>Last Verified</strong>: 2026-01-16
<strong>Source</strong>: <a href="https://github.com/tailwindlabs/tailwindcss">tailwindlabs/tailwindcss</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Quick Reference</h2><a id="user-content-quick-reference" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Quick Reference" href="#quick-reference"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">v4 Entry Point</h3><a id="user-content-v4-entry-point" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: v4 Entry Point" href="#v4-entry-point"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@import &quot;tailwindcss&quot;;"><pre><span class="pl-k">@import</span> <span class="pl-s">"tailwindcss"</span>;</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Key Directives</h3><a id="user-content-key-directives" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Key Directives" href="#key-directives"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Directive</th>
<th>Purpose</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>@theme</code></td>
<td>Define design tokens (colors, spacing, fonts)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>@utility</code></td>
<td>Create custom utility classes</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>@variant</code></td>
<td>Define custom variants (hover, focus, etc.)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>@source</code></td>
<td>Control class detection and safelisting</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>@reference</code></td>
<td>Import for @apply without emitting CSS</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Setup &amp; Configuration</h2><a id="user-content-setup--configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Setup &amp; Configuration" href="#setup--configuration"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">CSS Entry Point</h2><a id="user-content-css-entry-point" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CSS Entry Point" href="#css-entry-point"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">v4 uses <code>@import 'tailwindcss'</code> as the single entry point. The v3 <code>@tailwind</code> directives cause errors.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Incorrect (v3 directives - will error in v4):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@tailwind base;
@tailwind components;
@tailwind utilities;"><pre><span class="pl-k">@tailwind</span> base;
<span class="pl-k">@tailwind</span> components;
<span class="pl-k">@tailwind</span> utilities;</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Correct (v4 single import):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@import 'tailwindcss';"><pre><span class="pl-k">@import</span> <span class="pl-s">'tailwindcss'</span>;</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>With options:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/* Set source scanning base */
@import 'tailwindcss' source('../src');

/* Add prefix */
@import 'tailwindcss' prefix(tw);

/* Disable auto-detection */
@import 'tailwindcss' source(none);"><pre><span class="pl-c">/* Set source scanning base */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@import</span> <span class="pl-s">'tailwindcss'</span> source(<span class="pl-s">'../src'</span>);

<span class="pl-c">/* Add prefix */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@import</span> <span class="pl-s">'tailwindcss'</span> prefix(tw);

<span class="pl-c">/* Disable auto-detection */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@import</span> <span class="pl-s">'tailwindcss'</span> source(none);</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/functions-and-directives" rel="nofollow">Functions and Directives</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Next.js App Router Integration</h2><a id="user-content-nextjs-app-router-integration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Next.js App Router Integration" href="#nextjs-app-router-integration"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">v4 works with Next.js via PostCSS or the experimental <code>@next/tailwind</code> integration.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Setup (PostCSS - recommended):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// postcss.config.mjs
export default {
  plugins: {
    '@tailwindcss/postcss': {},
  },
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// postcss.config.mjs</span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> <span class="pl-k">default</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">plugins</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-s">'@tailwindcss/postcss'</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/* app/globals.css */
@import 'tailwindcss';

@source '../components/**/*.tsx';
@source '../app/**/*.tsx';"><pre><span class="pl-c">/* app/globals.css */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@import</span> <span class="pl-s">'tailwindcss'</span>;

<span class="pl-k">@source</span> <span class="pl-s">'../components/**/*.tsx'</span>;
<span class="pl-k">@source</span> <span class="pl-s">'../app/**/*.tsx'</span>;</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-tsx notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// app/layout.tsx
import './globals.css'

export default function RootLayout({ children }) {
  return (
    &lt;html lang=&quot;en&quot;&gt;
      &lt;body&gt;{children}&lt;/body&gt;
    &lt;/html&gt;
  )
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// app/layout.tsx</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s">'./globals.css'</span>

<span class="pl-k">export</span> <span class="pl-k">default</span> <span class="pl-k">function</span> <span class="pl-v">RootLayout</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span> children <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-kos">(</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span><span class="pl-s1">html</span> <span class="pl-c1">lang</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"en"</span><span class="pl-c1">&gt;</span>
      <span class="pl-c1">&lt;</span><span class="pl-s1">body</span><span class="pl-c1">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-s1">children</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-s1">body</span><span class="pl-c1">&gt;</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-s1">html</span><span class="pl-c1">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">)</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Incorrect (v3 config reference):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// tailwind.config.js - NOT the v4 way
module.exports = {
  content: ['./app/**/*.tsx', './components/**/*.tsx'],
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// tailwind.config.js - NOT the v4 way</span>
<span class="pl-smi">module</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">exports</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">content</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">'./app/**/*.tsx'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">'./components/**/*.tsx'</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Correct (v4 CSS source detection):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/* In globals.css */
@import 'tailwindcss';

/* Explicit source paths if auto-detection misses files */
@source '../components/**/*.tsx';"><pre><span class="pl-c">/* In globals.css */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@import</span> <span class="pl-s">'tailwindcss'</span>;

<span class="pl-c">/* Explicit source paths if auto-detection misses files */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@source</span> <span class="pl-s">'../components/**/*.tsx'</span>;</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Turbopack compatibility:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# Works with Turbopack
next dev --turbo"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Works with Turbopack</span>
next dev --turbo</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/installation/nextjs" rel="nofollow">Installation - Next.js</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Vite Integration</h2><a id="user-content-vite-integration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Vite Integration" href="#vite-integration"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">v4 has a dedicated Vite plugin that's faster than PostCSS. Use <code>@tailwindcss/vite</code> instead of <code>postcss.config.js</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Incorrect (v3 PostCSS approach):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// postcss.config.js - SLOWER in v4
export default {
  plugins: {
    tailwindcss: {},
    autoprefixer: {},
  }
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// postcss.config.js - SLOWER in v4</span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> <span class="pl-k">default</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">plugins</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">tailwindcss</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">autoprefixer</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Correct (v4 Vite plugin):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// vite.config.ts
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite'
import { defineConfig } from 'vite'

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [tailwindcss()],
})"><pre><span class="pl-c">// vite.config.ts</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">tailwindcss</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'@tailwindcss/vite'</span>
<span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-s1">defineConfig</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span> <span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s">'vite'</span>

<span class="pl-k">export</span> <span class="pl-k">default</span> <span class="pl-en">defineConfig</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">plugins</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-en">tailwindcss</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/* src/index.css */
@import 'tailwindcss';"><pre><span class="pl-c">/* src/index.css */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@import</span> <span class="pl-s">'tailwindcss'</span>;</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>If you must use PostCSS:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// postcss.config.js
export default {
  plugins: {
    '@tailwindcss/postcss': {}  // NEW package name
  }
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// postcss.config.js</span>
<span class="pl-k">export</span> <span class="pl-k">default</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">plugins</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-s">'@tailwindcss/postcss'</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span>  <span class="pl-c">// NEW package name</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Key differences:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>@tailwindcss/vite</code> - Fastest, uses Lightning CSS</li>
<li><code>@tailwindcss/postcss</code> - For non-Vite builds</li>
<li>No <code>autoprefixer</code> needed - v4 handles prefixes</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/installation/vite" rel="nofollow">Installation - Vite</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Source Detection and Safelisting</h2><a id="user-content-source-detection-and-safelisting" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Source Detection and Safelisting" href="#source-detection-and-safelisting"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">v4 auto-detects source files but provides <code>@source</code> for manual control. Safelisting uses <code>@source inline()</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Add paths:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@source &quot;../shared-components/**/*.tsx&quot;;
@source &quot;./templates/*.html&quot;;"><pre><span class="pl-k">@source</span> <span class="pl-s">"../shared-components/**/*.tsx"</span>;
<span class="pl-k">@source</span> <span class="pl-s">"./templates/*.html"</span>;</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Exclude paths:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@source not &quot;./legacy/**/*&quot;;
@source not &quot;../packages/deprecated&quot;;"><pre><span class="pl-k">@source</span> <span class="pl-c1">not</span> <span class="pl-s">"./legacy/**/*"</span>;
<span class="pl-k">@source</span> <span class="pl-c1">not</span> <span class="pl-s">"../packages/deprecated"</span>;</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Safelist specific classes:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/* Safelist exact classes */
@source inline(&quot;bg-red-500 text-white p-4&quot;);

/* With brace expansion */
@source inline(&quot;bg-{red,blue,green}-{100,500,900}&quot;);

/* With variants */
@source inline(&quot;hover:bg-red-500 dark:text-white&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-c">/* Safelist exact classes */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@source</span> inline(<span class="pl-s">"bg-red-500 text-white p-4"</span>);

<span class="pl-c">/* With brace expansion */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@source</span> inline(<span class="pl-s">"bg-{red,blue,green}-{100,500,900}"</span>);

<span class="pl-c">/* With variants */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@source</span> inline(<span class="pl-s">"hover:bg-red-500 dark:text-white"</span>);</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Incorrect (v3 safelist in JS - not supported):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// tailwind.config.js - THIS DOES NOT WORK IN V4
module.exports = {
  safelist: ['bg-red-500', 'text-white']  // Not supported!
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// tailwind.config.js - THIS DOES NOT WORK IN V4</span>
<span class="pl-smi">module</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">exports</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">safelist</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span><span class="pl-s">'bg-red-500'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">'text-white'</span><span class="pl-kos">]</span>  <span class="pl-c">// Not supported!</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Correct (v4 @source inline):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@source inline(&quot;bg-red-500 text-white&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-k">@source</span> inline(<span class="pl-s">"bg-red-500 text-white"</span>);</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Important:</strong> Tailwind scans files as plain text. String interpolation won't work:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-tsx notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// ❌ These classes WON'T be detected
const color = 'red'
className={`bg-${color}-500`}

// ✅ Use complete class names
className={color === 'red' ? 'bg-red-500' : 'bg-blue-500'}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// ❌ These classes WON'T be detected</span>
<span class="pl-k">const</span> <span class="pl-s1">color</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">'red'</span>
<span class="pl-s1">className</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span><span class="pl-kos"></span><span class="pl-s">`bg-<span class="pl-s1"><span class="pl-kos">${</span><span class="pl-s1">color</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></span>-500`</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span>

<span class="pl-c">// ✅ Use complete class names</span>
<span class="pl-s1">className</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-kos">{</span>color <span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">'red'</span> ? <span class="pl-s">'bg-red-500'</span> : <span class="pl-s">'bg-blue-500'</span><span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/detecting-classes-in-source-files" rel="nofollow">Detecting Classes</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Theme Configuration with @theme</h2><a id="user-content-theme-configuration-with-theme" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Theme Configuration with @theme" href="#theme-configuration-with-theme"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">v4 uses <code>@theme</code> blocks in CSS to define design tokens. This replaces the JS-based <code>tailwind.config.js</code> for most use cases.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Incorrect (v3 JS config as primary):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// tailwind.config.js - still works but not the v4 way
module.exports = {
  theme: {
    extend: {
      colors: {
        primary: '#3b82f6',
      }
    }
  }
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// tailwind.config.js - still works but not the v4 way</span>
<span class="pl-smi">module</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">exports</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">theme</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
    <span class="pl-c1">extend</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
      <span class="pl-c1">colors</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
        <span class="pl-c1">primary</span>: <span class="pl-s">'#3b82f6'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
      <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Correct (v4 CSS-first):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@import 'tailwindcss';

@theme {
  --color-primary: #3b82f6;
  --color-secondary: #64748b;
  --breakpoint-3xl: 1920px;
  --font-display: &quot;Satoshi&quot;, sans-serif;
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">@import</span> <span class="pl-s">'tailwindcss'</span>;

<span class="pl-k">@theme</span> {
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-primary</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-pds"><span class="pl-kos">#</span>3b82f6</span>;
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-secondary</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-pds"><span class="pl-kos">#</span>64748b</span>;
  <span class="pl-s1">--breakpoint-3xl</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-c1">1920<span class="pl-smi">px</span></span>;
  <span class="pl-s1">--font-display</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-s">"Satoshi"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> sans-serif;
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Override entire namespace:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@theme {
  /* Remove all default colors */
  --color-*: initial;

  /* Define only your colors */
  --color-brand: #ff6b00;
  --color-surface: #fafafa;
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">@theme</span> {
  <span class="pl-c">/* Remove all default colors */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-</span><span class="pl-c1">*</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> initial;

  <span class="pl-c">/* Define only your colors */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-brand</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-pds"><span class="pl-kos">#</span>ff6b00</span>;
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-surface</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-pds"><span class="pl-kos">#</span>fafafa</span>;
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Reference variables (use @theme inline):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@theme inline {
  /* Can reference other variables */
  --color-primary-light: oklch(from var(--color-primary) calc(l + 0.1) c h);
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">@theme</span> inline {
  <span class="pl-c">/* Can reference other variables */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-primary-light</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-en">oklch</span>(from <span class="pl-en">var</span>(<span class="pl-s1">--color-primary</span>) <span class="pl-en">calc</span>(l <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-c1">0.1</span>) c h);
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/theme" rel="nofollow">Theme Variables</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Migration from v3</h2><a id="user-content-migration-from-v3" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Migration from v3" href="#migration-from-v3"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Native Container Queries</h2><a id="user-content-native-container-queries" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Native Container Queries" href="#native-container-queries"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">v4 includes container queries natively. Remove <code>@tailwindcss/container-queries</code> plugin.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Incorrect (v3 with plugin):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// tailwind.config.js
module.exports = {
  plugins: [
    require('@tailwindcss/container-queries'),  // NOT needed in v4
  ],
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// tailwind.config.js</span>
<span class="pl-smi">module</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">exports</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">plugins</span>: <span class="pl-kos">[</span>
    <span class="pl-en">require</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">'@tailwindcss/container-queries'</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>  <span class="pl-c">// NOT needed in v4</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">]</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Correct (v4 native):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;!-- Define container --&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;@container&quot;&gt;
  &lt;!-- Respond to container width --&gt;
  &lt;div class=&quot;@lg:grid-cols-2 @md:flex-row&quot;&gt;
    Content
  &lt;/div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- Define container --&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">@container</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- Respond to container width --&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">@lg:grid-cols-2 @md:flex-row</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
    Content
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Container variants:</strong></p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Variant</th>
<th>Container Width</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>@sm:</code></td>
<td>320px (20rem)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>@md:</code></td>
<td>448px (28rem)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>@lg:</code></td>
<td>512px (32rem)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>@xl:</code></td>
<td>576px (36rem)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>@2xl:</code></td>
<td>672px (42rem)</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Named containers:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;div class=&quot;@container/sidebar&quot;&gt;
  &lt;div class=&quot;@lg/sidebar:hidden&quot;&gt;
    Hidden when sidebar is large
  &lt;/div&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">@container/sidebar</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">@lg/sidebar:hidden</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
    Hidden when sidebar is large
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Common confusion:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>lg:</code> = viewport breakpoint (screen width)</li>
<li><code>@lg:</code> = container query (parent width)</li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;!-- Viewport-based (screen width) --&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;lg:hidden&quot;&gt;Hidden on large screens&lt;/div&gt;

&lt;!-- Container-based (parent width) --&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;@lg:hidden&quot;&gt;Hidden in large containers&lt;/div&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- Viewport-based (screen width) --&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">lg:hidden</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Hidden on large screens<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>

<span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- Container-based (parent width) --&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">@lg:hidden</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Hidden in large containers<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/container-queries" rel="nofollow">Container Queries</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Dark Mode Configuration</h2><a id="user-content-dark-mode-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Dark Mode Configuration" href="#dark-mode-configuration"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">v4 defaults to <code>media</code> strategy (prefers-color-scheme). For class-based dark mode, use <code>@variant</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>v4 default (media query):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/* Automatic - follows system preference */
@import 'tailwindcss';

/* dark: variants use @media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) */"><pre><span class="pl-c">/* Automatic - follows system preference */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@import</span> <span class="pl-s">'tailwindcss'</span>;

<span class="pl-c">/* dark: variants use @media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) */</span></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;div class=&quot;bg-white dark:bg-gray-900&quot;&gt;
  Follows system preference
&lt;/div&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-white dark:bg-gray-900</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  Follows system preference
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Class-based dark mode:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@import 'tailwindcss';

@variant dark (&amp;:is(.dark, .dark *));"><pre><span class="pl-k">@import</span> <span class="pl-s">'tailwindcss'</span>;

<span class="pl-k">@variant</span> dark (<span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span><span class="pl-c1">is</span>(.<span class="pl-c1">dark</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> .<span class="pl-c1">dark</span> <span class="pl-ent"><span class="pl-c1">*</span></span>));</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;html class=&quot;dark&quot;&gt;
  &lt;body&gt;
    &lt;div class=&quot;bg-white dark:bg-gray-900&quot;&gt;
      Dark when .dark class on html
    &lt;/div&gt;
  &lt;/body&gt;
&lt;/html&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">dark</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">body</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-white dark:bg-gray-900</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
      Dark when .dark class on html
    <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">body</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Data attribute strategy:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@variant dark (&amp;:is([data-theme=&quot;dark&quot;], [data-theme=&quot;dark&quot;] *));"><pre><span class="pl-k">@variant</span> dark (<span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span><span class="pl-c1">is</span>([<span class="pl-c1">data-theme</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"dark"</span>]<span class="pl-kos">,</span> [<span class="pl-c1">data-theme</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"dark"</span>] <span class="pl-ent"><span class="pl-c1">*</span></span>));</pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;html data-theme=&quot;dark&quot;&gt;
  &lt;div class=&quot;dark:bg-gray-900&quot;&gt;Dark mode active&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;/html&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span> <span class="pl-c1">data-theme</span>="<span class="pl-s">dark</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">dark:bg-gray-900</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Dark mode active<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Incorrect (v3 config):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// tailwind.config.js - NOT the v4 way
module.exports = {
  darkMode: 'class',  // Use @variant instead
}"><pre><span class="pl-c">// tailwind.config.js - NOT the v4 way</span>
<span class="pl-smi">module</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-c1">exports</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-c1">darkMode</span>: <span class="pl-s">'class'</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>  <span class="pl-c">// Use <span class="pl-k">@variant</span> instead</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Correct (v4 @variant):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@variant dark (&amp;:is(.dark, .dark *));"><pre><span class="pl-k">@variant</span> dark (<span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span><span class="pl-c1">is</span>(.<span class="pl-c1">dark</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> .<span class="pl-c1">dark</span> <span class="pl-ent"><span class="pl-c1">*</span></span>));</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/dark-mode" rel="nofollow">Dark Mode</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Explicit var() in Arbitrary Values</h2><a id="user-content-explicit-var-in-arbitrary-values" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Explicit var() in Arbitrary Values" href="#explicit-var-in-arbitrary-values"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">v4 requires explicit <code>var()</code> when using CSS variables in arbitrary values. The v3 implicit injection is removed.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Incorrect (v3 implicit var):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;div class=&quot;bg-[--my-color]&quot;&gt;Colored box&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;!-- In v4, this produces NO CSS output - silently fails --&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-[--my-color]</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Colored box<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- In v4, this produces NO CSS output - silently fails --&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Correct (v4 explicit var):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;div class=&quot;bg-[var(--my-color)]&quot;&gt;Colored box&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;!-- Correctly references the CSS variable --&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-[var(--my-color)]</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Colored box<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- Correctly references the CSS variable --&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This applies to all arbitrary values that reference CSS variables:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;!-- All incorrect in v4 --&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;w-[--sidebar-width]&quot;&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;p-[--spacing-lg]&quot;&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;text-[--heading-size]&quot;&gt;

&lt;!-- All correct in v4 --&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;w-[var(--sidebar-width)]&quot;&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;p-[var(--spacing-lg)]&quot;&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;text-[var(--heading-size)]&quot;&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- All incorrect in v4 --&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">w-[--sidebar-width]</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">p-[--spacing-lg]</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">text-[--heading-size]</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>

<span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- All correct in v4 --&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">w-[var(--sidebar-width)]</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">p-[var(--spacing-lg)]</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">text-[var(--heading-size)]</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/upgrade-guide" rel="nofollow">Upgrade Guide</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">OKLCH Color Space</h2><a id="user-content-oklch-color-space" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OKLCH Color Space" href="#oklch-color-space"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">v4 uses OKLCH color space by default. Colors appear more vibrant but may look different from v3.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>What changed:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/* v3 output */
.bg-blue-500 { background-color: rgb(59 130 246); }

/* v4 output */
.bg-blue-500 { background-color: oklch(0.623 0.214 259.815); }"><pre><span class="pl-c">/* v3 output */</span>
.<span class="pl-c1">bg-blue-500</span> { <span class="pl-c1">background-color</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-en">rgb</span>(<span class="pl-c1">59</span> <span class="pl-c1">130</span> <span class="pl-c1">246</span>); }

<span class="pl-c">/* v4 output */</span>
.<span class="pl-c1">bg-blue-500</span> { <span class="pl-c1">background-color</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-en">oklch</span>(<span class="pl-c1">0.623</span> <span class="pl-c1">0.214</span> <span class="pl-c1">259.815</span>); }</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Opacity still works:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;!-- 50% opacity --&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;bg-blue-500/50&quot;&gt;Semi-transparent&lt;/div&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- 50% opacity --&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-blue-500/50</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Semi-transparent<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/* v4 output */
.bg-blue-500\/50 { background-color: oklch(0.623 0.214 259.815 / 0.5); }"><pre><span class="pl-c">/* v4 output */</span>
.<span class="pl-c1">bg-blue-500\/</span>50 { <span class="pl-c1">background-color</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-en">oklch</span>(<span class="pl-c1">0.623</span> <span class="pl-c1">0.214</span> <span class="pl-c1">259.815</span> <span class="pl-c1">/</span> <span class="pl-c1">0.5</span>); }</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Gradients look different:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;!-- Gradient interpolation uses oklch --&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;bg-gradient-to-r from-red-500 to-blue-500&quot;&gt;
  &lt;!-- v4: smoother, more vibrant transition --&gt;
  &lt;!-- v3: may have muddy middle colors --&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- Gradient interpolation uses oklch --&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-gradient-to-r from-red-500 to-blue-500</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- v4: smoother, more vibrant transition --&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- v3: may have muddy middle colors --&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Custom colors with oklch:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@theme {
  --color-brand: oklch(0.7 0.15 250);
  --color-brand-light: oklch(from var(--color-brand) calc(l + 0.1) c h);
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">@theme</span> {
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-brand</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-en">oklch</span>(<span class="pl-c1">0.7</span> <span class="pl-c1">0.15</span> <span class="pl-c1">250</span>);
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-brand-light</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-en">oklch</span>(from <span class="pl-en">var</span>(<span class="pl-s1">--color-brand</span>) <span class="pl-en">calc</span>(l <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-c1">0.1</span>) c h);
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>P3 wide gamut:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@theme {
  /* Wide gamut color (brighter than sRGB) */
  --color-vivid: oklch(0.8 0.3 150);
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">@theme</span> {
  <span class="pl-c">/* Wide gamut color (brighter than sRGB) */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-vivid</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-en">oklch</span>(<span class="pl-c1">0.8</span> <span class="pl-c1">0.3</span> <span class="pl-c1">150</span>);
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>If you need exact v3 colors:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@theme {
  /* Override with rgb if needed */
  --color-blue-500: rgb(59 130 246);
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">@theme</span> {
  <span class="pl-c">/* Override with rgb if needed */</span>
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-blue-500</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-en">rgb</span>(<span class="pl-c1">59</span> <span class="pl-c1">130</span> <span class="pl-c1">246</span>);
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/colors" rel="nofollow">Colors</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Ring Requires Explicit Width</h2><a id="user-content-ring-requires-explicit-width" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Ring Requires Explicit Width" href="#ring-requires-explicit-width"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">v4 changed <code>ring</code> to require an explicit width. The default <code>ring</code> utility no longer applies a visible ring.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Incorrect (v3 mental model):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;button class=&quot;focus:ring&quot;&gt;Focusable button&lt;/button&gt;
&lt;!-- In v4, this produces a ring with width 0 - invisible --&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">button</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">focus:ring</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Focusable button<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">button</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- In v4, this produces a ring with width 0 - invisible --&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Correct (v4 explicit width):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;button class=&quot;focus:ring-3&quot;&gt;Focusable button&lt;/button&gt;
&lt;!-- Produces a 3px ring, similar to v3's default &quot;ring&quot; --&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">button</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">focus:ring-3</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Focusable button<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">button</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- Produces a 3px ring, similar to v3's default "ring" --&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Available widths:</strong> <code>ring-0</code>, <code>ring-1</code>, <code>ring-2</code>, <code>ring-3</code>, <code>ring-4</code>, <code>ring-8</code></p>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/upgrade-guide" rel="nofollow">Upgrade Guide</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Shadow Scale Shift</h2><a id="user-content-shadow-scale-shift" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Shadow Scale Shift" href="#shadow-scale-shift"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">v4 shifted the shadow scale down by one step. What was <code>shadow</code> in v3 is now <code>shadow-sm</code> in v4.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Incorrect (v3 mental model):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;div class=&quot;shadow&quot;&gt;Card with shadow&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;!-- In v4, this produces a SMALLER shadow than v3's &quot;shadow&quot; --&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">shadow</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Card with shadow<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- In v4, this produces a SMALLER shadow than v3's "shadow" --&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Correct (v4 equivalent):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;div class=&quot;shadow-sm&quot;&gt;Card with shadow&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;!-- Matches v3's &quot;shadow&quot; visual output --&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">shadow-sm</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Card with shadow<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- Matches v3's "shadow" visual output --&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Full scale mapping:</strong></p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>v3 Class</th>
<th>v4 Equivalent</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>shadow-sm</code></td>
<td><code>shadow-xs</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>shadow</code></td>
<td><code>shadow-sm</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>shadow-md</code></td>
<td><code>shadow</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>shadow-lg</code></td>
<td><code>shadow-md</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>shadow-xl</code></td>
<td><code>shadow-lg</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>shadow-2xl</code></td>
<td><code>shadow-xl</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/upgrade-guide" rel="nofollow">Upgrade Guide</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Customization</h2><a id="user-content-customization" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Customization" href="#customization"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Custom Variants</h2><a id="user-content-custom-variants" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Custom Variants" href="#custom-variants"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use <code>@custom-variant</code> for advanced selector logic beyond built-in variants.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Dark mode with class strategy:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@custom-variant dark (&amp;:is(.dark *));"><pre><span class="pl-k">@custom-variant</span> dark (<span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span><span class="pl-c1">is</span>(.<span class="pl-c1">dark</span> <span class="pl-ent"><span class="pl-c1">*</span></span>));</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This creates a <code>dark:</code> variant that matches elements inside a <code>.dark</code> parent.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Usage:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;html class=&quot;dark&quot;&gt;
  &lt;body class=&quot;bg-white dark:bg-black&quot;&gt;
    &lt;!-- dark:bg-black applies --&gt;
  &lt;/body&gt;
&lt;/html&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">dark</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">body</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-white dark:bg-black</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
    <span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- dark:bg-black applies --&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">body</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Multiple selectors:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@custom-variant hocus (&amp;:hover, &amp;:focus);
@custom-variant group-hocus (:merge(.group):hover &amp;, :merge(.group):focus &amp;);"><pre><span class="pl-k">@custom-variant</span> hocus (<span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span>hover<span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span>focus);
<span class="pl-k">@custom-variant</span> group-hocus (<span class="pl-kos">:</span>merge(.group)<span class="pl-kos">:</span>hover <span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-kos">:</span>merge(.group)<span class="pl-kos">:</span>focus <span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span>);</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Print variant:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@custom-variant print (@media print);"><pre><span class="pl-k">@custom-variant</span> print (<span class="pl-k">@media</span> print);</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>vs @variant:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/* @variant - simple selector list */
@variant hocus (&amp;:hover, &amp;:focus);

/* @custom-variant - same syntax, preferred for clarity */
@custom-variant hocus (&amp;:hover, &amp;:focus);"><pre><span class="pl-c">/* @variant - simple selector list */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@variant</span> hocus (<span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span>hover<span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span>focus);

<span class="pl-c">/* @custom-variant - same syntax, preferred for clarity */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@custom-variant</span> hocus (<span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span>hover<span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span>focus);</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Both work identically. <code>@custom-variant</code> is more explicit about intent.</p>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/adding-custom-styles#adding-custom-variants" rel="nofollow">Custom Variants</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">New Gradient Syntax</h2><a id="user-content-new-gradient-syntax" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: New Gradient Syntax" href="#new-gradient-syntax"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Tailwind v4 introduces cleaner gradient utilities.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Linear gradients:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;!-- v4 syntax --&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;bg-linear-to-r from-blue-500 to-purple-500&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;bg-linear-to-b from-foreground to-foreground/70&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;

&lt;!-- Direction variants --&gt;
bg-linear-to-t    &lt;!-- top --&gt;
bg-linear-to-tr   &lt;!-- top-right --&gt;
bg-linear-to-r    &lt;!-- right --&gt;
bg-linear-to-br   &lt;!-- bottom-right --&gt;
bg-linear-to-b    &lt;!-- bottom --&gt;
bg-linear-to-bl   &lt;!-- bottom-left --&gt;
bg-linear-to-l    &lt;!-- left --&gt;
bg-linear-to-tl   &lt;!-- top-left --&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- v4 syntax --&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-linear-to-r from-blue-500 to-purple-500</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-linear-to-b from-foreground to-foreground/70</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>

<span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- Direction variants --&gt;</span>
bg-linear-to-t    <span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- top --&gt;</span>
bg-linear-to-tr   <span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- top-right --&gt;</span>
bg-linear-to-r    <span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- right --&gt;</span>
bg-linear-to-br   <span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- bottom-right --&gt;</span>
bg-linear-to-b    <span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- bottom --&gt;</span>
bg-linear-to-bl   <span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- bottom-left --&gt;</span>
bg-linear-to-l    <span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- left --&gt;</span>
bg-linear-to-tl   <span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- top-left --&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>vs v3 syntax:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;!-- v3 (still works) --&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-500 to-purple-500&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;

&lt;!-- v4 preferred --&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;bg-linear-to-r from-blue-500 to-purple-500&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- v3 (still works) --&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-500 to-purple-500</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>

<span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- v4 preferred --&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-linear-to-r from-blue-500 to-purple-500</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Radial gradients:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;div class=&quot;bg-radial from-white to-transparent&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;bg-radial-[at_top] from-sky-500 to-transparent&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-radial from-white to-transparent</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-radial-[at_top] from-sky-500 to-transparent</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Conic gradients:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;div class=&quot;bg-conic from-red-500 via-yellow-500 to-red-500&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-conic from-red-500 via-yellow-500 to-red-500</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>With arbitrary values:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;div class=&quot;bg-linear-[45deg] from-pink-500 to-orange-500&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;
&lt;div class=&quot;bg-radial-[circle_at_top] from-blue-500 to-transparent&quot;&gt;&lt;/div&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-linear-[45deg] from-pink-500 to-orange-500</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">bg-radial-[circle_at_top] from-blue-500 to-transparent</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Text gradients:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=".text-gradient {
  @apply bg-clip-text text-transparent bg-linear-to-b from-foreground to-foreground/70;
}"><pre>.<span class="pl-c1">text-gradient</span> {
  <span class="pl-k">@apply</span> bg-clip-text text-transparent bg-linear-to-b from-foreground to-foreground/70;
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/background-image" rel="nofollow">Gradients</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Theme Inline Modifier</h2><a id="user-content-theme-inline-modifier" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Theme Inline Modifier" href="#theme-inline-modifier"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use <code>@theme inline</code> to preserve CSS variables in output alongside utilities.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Standard @theme:</strong></p>
