<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@theme {
  --font-sans: &quot;Inter&quot;, sans-serif;
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">@theme</span> {
  <span class="pl-s1">--font-sans</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-s">"Inter"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> sans-serif;
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Variables used for utility generation but NOT emitted as CSS variables.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>With inline modifier:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@theme inline {
  --font-sans: &quot;SF Pro Text&quot;, system-ui, sans-serif;
  --color-primary: #0066cc;
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">@theme</span> inline {
  <span class="pl-s1">--font-sans</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-s">"SF Pro Text"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> system-ui<span class="pl-kos">,</span> sans-serif;
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-primary</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-pds"><span class="pl-kos">#</span>0066cc</span>;
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Variables ARE emitted as CSS custom properties AND used for utilities.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Output difference:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/* @theme (no inline) - only utilities generated */
.font-sans { font-family: Inter, sans-serif; }

/* @theme inline - variables preserved */
:root {
  --font-sans: SF Pro Text, system-ui, sans-serif;
  --color-primary: #0066cc;
}
.font-sans { font-family: var(--font-sans); }"><pre><span class="pl-c">/* @theme (no inline) - only utilities generated */</span>
.<span class="pl-c1">font-sans</span> { <span class="pl-c1">font-family</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> Inter<span class="pl-kos">,</span> sans-serif; }

<span class="pl-c">/* @theme inline - variables preserved */</span>
<span class="pl-kos">:</span><span class="pl-c1">root</span> {
  <span class="pl-s1">--font-sans</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> SF Pro Text<span class="pl-kos">,</span> system-ui<span class="pl-kos">,</span> sans-serif;
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-primary</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-pds"><span class="pl-kos">#</span>0066cc</span>;
}
.<span class="pl-c1">font-sans</span> { <span class="pl-c1">font-family</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-en">var</span>(<span class="pl-s1">--font-sans</span>); }</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>When to use inline:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Shadcn/Radix design systems that reference CSS vars</li>
<li>Dynamic theming with JavaScript</li>
<li>Sharing variables between Tailwind and custom CSS</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Combining with :root:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@theme inline {
  --color-background: var(--background);
  --color-foreground: var(--foreground);
}

:root {
  --background: #ffffff;
  --foreground: #1d1d1f;
}

.dark {
  --background: #000000;
  --foreground: #f5f5f7;
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">@theme</span> inline {
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-background</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-en">var</span>(<span class="pl-s1">--background</span>);
  <span class="pl-s1">--color-foreground</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-en">var</span>(<span class="pl-s1">--foreground</span>);
}

<span class="pl-kos">:</span><span class="pl-c1">root</span> {
  <span class="pl-s1">--background</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-pds"><span class="pl-kos">#</span>ffffff</span>;
  <span class="pl-s1">--foreground</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-pds"><span class="pl-kos">#</span>1d1d1f</span>;
}

.<span class="pl-c1">dark</span> {
  <span class="pl-s1">--background</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-pds"><span class="pl-kos">#</span>000000</span>;
  <span class="pl-s1">--foreground</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-pds"><span class="pl-kos">#</span>f5f5f7</span>;
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/theme" rel="nofollow">Theme Configuration</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Custom Utilities with @utility</h2><a id="user-content-custom-utilities-with-utility" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Custom Utilities with @utility" href="#custom-utilities-with-utility"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">v4 uses <code>@utility</code> directive to define custom utilities that work with variants. This replaces the v3 <code>@layer utilities</code> pattern.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Incorrect (v3 @layer pattern):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@layer utilities {
  .content-auto {
    content-visibility: auto;
  }
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">@layer</span> utilities {
  .<span class="pl-c1">content-auto</span> {
    <span class="pl-c1">content-visibility</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> auto;
  }
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Correct (v4 @utility directive):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@utility content-auto {
  content-visibility: auto;
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">@utility</span> content-auto {
  <span class="pl-c1">content-visibility</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> auto;
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>With variants - they just work:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;div class=&quot;hover:content-auto md:content-auto&quot;&gt;
  &lt;!-- Variants apply automatically --&gt;
&lt;/div&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">hover:content-auto md:content-auto</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-c">&lt;!-- Variants apply automatically --&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">div</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Functional utility with values:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@utility tab-* {
  tab-size: --value(--tab-size-*, integer);
}

/* Usage: tab-2, tab-4, tab-8 based on --tab-size-* variables */"><pre><span class="pl-k">@utility</span> tab-<span class="pl-c1">*</span> {
  <span class="pl-c1">tab-size</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> <span class="pl-en">--value</span>(<span class="pl-s1">--tab-size-*</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> integer);
}

<span class="pl-c">/* Usage: tab-2, tab-4, tab-8 based on --tab-size-* variables */</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/functions-and-directives" rel="nofollow">Functions and Directives</a></p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Custom Variants with @variant</h2><a id="user-content-custom-variants-with-variant" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Custom Variants with @variant" href="#custom-variants-with-variant"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">v4 uses <code>@variant</code> to define custom variants directly in CSS.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Correct (v4 @variant):</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="/* Simple variant - combines selectors */
@variant hocus (&amp;:hover, &amp;:focus);

/* Media query variant */
@variant pointer-coarse (@media (pointer: coarse));

/* Supports query */
@variant supports-grid (@supports (display: grid));

/* Dark mode with class strategy */
@variant dark (&amp;:is(.dark, .dark *));"><pre><span class="pl-c">/* Simple variant - combines selectors */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@variant</span> hocus (<span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span>hover<span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span>focus);

<span class="pl-c">/* Media query variant */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@variant</span> pointer-coarse (<span class="pl-k">@media</span> (<span class="pl-c1">pointer</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> coarse));

<span class="pl-c">/* Supports query */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@variant</span> supports-grid (<span class="pl-k">@supports</span> (<span class="pl-c1">display</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span> grid));

<span class="pl-c">/* Dark mode with class strategy */</span>
<span class="pl-k">@variant</span> dark (<span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span><span class="pl-c1">is</span>(.<span class="pl-c1">dark</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> .<span class="pl-c1">dark</span> <span class="pl-ent"><span class="pl-c1">*</span></span>));</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Usage:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;button class=&quot;hocus:bg-blue-600 pointer-coarse:p-4&quot;&gt;
  Hover or focus me
&lt;/button&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">button</span> <span class="pl-c1">class</span>="<span class="pl-s">hocus:bg-blue-600 pointer-coarse:p-4</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  Hover or focus me
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">button</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Nested variant with @slot:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-css notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="@variant dark {
  &amp;:where([data-theme=&quot;dark&quot;], [data-theme=&quot;dark&quot;] *) {
    @slot;
  }
}"><pre><span class="pl-k">@variant</span> dark {
  <span class="pl-ent">&amp;</span><span class="pl-kos">:</span><span class="pl-c1">where</span>([<span class="pl-c1">data-theme</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"dark"</span>]<span class="pl-kos">,</span> [<span class="pl-c1">data-theme</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"dark"</span>] <span class="pl-ent"><span class="pl-c1">*</span></span>) {
    <span class="pl-k">@slot</span>;
  }
}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Reference: <a href="https://tailwindcss.com/docs/functions-and-directives" rel="nofollow">Functions and Directives</a></p>
